你是Ascend910B设备算子优化角色。不要调用工具；只从冻结的分块/调度空间选择一个新配置。

输出JSON：config（所有配置字段）、hypothesis（中文机制假设）、predictions（待验证指标与变化数组）。

配置将由相同的通用变换器生成完整候选，不修改原数学内核。两个组拥有相同的空间。

矩阵配置：bm=32/64/128，bn=32/64/128，bk32=32/64/128/256，bk16=64/128/256/512，group=0/2/4/8，multi为bool。

归约配置：bo=2/4/8/16/32，last_br=128/256/512/1024/2048，bc=64/128/256/512/1024，middle_br=4/8/16/32/64，native/multi为bool。

仅输出当前算子的字段；native仅影响FP16，BF16/FP32仍为FP32中间值。mask由整除条件自动确定，不准去掉非整除边界。

归约middle_br表示FP32/BF16中间归约片；FP16原生中间值的实际BR为2×middle_br。BO/last_br只影响2D末轴；BC/middle_br只影响3D中间轴。

同组共两次候选，第二次须不同于此前配置。失败和回退不能当成更快设备路径。

为避开历史单K迭代流水超时，正式矩阵新路径要求K至少是2个BK；其他情况走共享兼容修复的父路径。

算子：amin

默认配置：{"bo": 8, "last_br": 1024, "bc": 512, "middle_br": 16, "native": true, "multi": true}

_device_parent=run
def run(inp,dim=None,keepdim=False):
    dims=[dim] if isinstance(dim,int) else dim
    if not (inp.is_contiguous() and inp.ndim in (2,3) and dims is not None
            and len(dims)==1 and dims[0]%inp.ndim==1 and inp.shape[1]>0
            and inp.numel()>0):
        return _device_parent(inp,dim=dim,keepdim=keepdim)
    shape=list(inp.shape);out_shape=shape[:]
    if keepdim:out_shape[1]=1
    else:out_shape.pop(1)
    out=torch.empty(out_shape,dtype=inp.dtype,device=inp.device)
    native=__NATIVE__ and inp.dtype==torch.float16
    with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
        if inp.ndim==2:
            bo,br=__BO__,__LAST_BR__
            masked=shape[0]%bo!=0 or shape[1]%br!=0
            amin_native_last_kernel[(triton.cdiv(shape[0],bo),)](inp,out,shape[0],shape[1],bo,br,native,masked,multibuffer=__MULTI__)
        else:
            bc,br=__BC__,__MIDDLE_BR__
            # 同一配置按中间值字节宽度组织；FP16原生路径的BR为基准两倍。
            if native:br=2*br
            masked=shape[2]%bc!=0 or shape[1]%br!=0
            amin_native_middle_kernel[(shape[0],triton.cdiv(shape[2],bc))](inp,out,shape[1],shape[2],bc,br,native,masked,multibuffer=__MULTI__)
    return out
amin=run


父版完整源码（含历史优化，数学计算不得改变）：
import logging
import math
import torch
import triton
import triton.language as tl
from flag_gems import runtime
from flag_gems.runtime import torch_device_fn
from flag_gems.utils import dim_compress, libentry, libtuner
from flag_gems.utils import triton_lang_extension as tle
from flag_gems.utils.limits import get_dtype_max
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

@triton.jit
def _hg_nan_min(a, b):
    return tl.minimum(a, b, propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)

@libentry()
@triton.jit
def amin_kernel_1(inp, mid, M, BLOCK_SIZE: tl.constexpr):
    pid = tle.program_id(0)
    offset = pid * BLOCK_SIZE + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_SIZE)
    inp_ptrs = inp + offset
    mask = offset < M
    max_value = get_dtype_max(inp.type.element_ty)
    inp_val = tl.load(inp_ptrs, mask=mask, other=max_value)
    amin_val = tl.reduce(inp_val, axis=None, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    mid_ptr = mid + pid
    tl.store(mid_ptr, amin_val)

@libentry()
@triton.jit
def amin_kernel_2(mid, out, mid_size, BLOCK_MID: tl.constexpr):
    offset = tl.arange(0, BLOCK_MID)
    mid_ptrs = mid + offset
    mask = offset < mid_size
    max_value = get_dtype_max(mid.type.element_ty)
    mid_val = tl.load(mid_ptrs, mask=mask, other=max_value)
    amin_val = tl.reduce(mid_val, axis=None, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    tl.store(out, amin_val)

@libentry()
@libtuner(configs=runtime.get_tuned_config('naive_reduction'), key=['M', 'N'])
@triton.jit
def amin_kernel(inp, out, M, N, BLOCK_M: tl.constexpr, BLOCK_N: tl.constexpr):
    dtype = inp.type.element_ty
    max_value = get_dtype_max(dtype)
    pid = tle.program_id(0)
    rows = pid * BLOCK_M + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_M)[:, None]
    inp = inp + rows * N
    out = out + rows
    row_mask = rows < M
    acc_type = tl.float32 if dtype is tl.bfloat16 else dtype
    _all = tl.full([BLOCK_M, BLOCK_N], value=max_value, dtype=acc_type)
    for off in range(0, N, BLOCK_N):
        cols = off + tl.arange(0, BLOCK_N)[None, :]
        col_mask = cols < N
        mask = row_mask and col_mask
        a = tl.load(inp + cols, mask, other=max_value)
        _all = tl.minimum(_all, a, propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    all = tl.reduce(_all, axis=1, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)[:, None]
    tl.store(out, all, row_mask)

def amin(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    logger.debug('GEMS AMIN')
    assert inp.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float16, torch.bfloat16), 'amin only supports float dtypes'
    if dim is None or len(dim) == 0:
        M = inp.numel()
        block_size = triton.next_power_of_2(math.ceil(math.sqrt(M)))
        mid_size = triton.cdiv(M, block_size)
        block_mid = triton.next_power_of_2(mid_size)
        dtype = inp.dtype
        mid = torch.empty((mid_size,), dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        if not keepdim:
            out = torch.empty([], dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        else:
            shape = list(inp.shape)
            for i in range(0, inp.dim()):
                shape[i] = 1
            out = torch.empty(shape, dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
            amin_kernel_1[mid_size, 1](inp, mid, M, block_size)
            amin_kernel_2[1, 1](mid, out, mid_size, block_mid)
        return out
    else:
        if isinstance(dim, int):
            dim = [dim]
        assert (i >= -inp.ndim and i < inp.ndim for i in dim), 'Invalid dim'
        dtype = inp.dtype
        shape = list(inp.shape)
        dim = [d % inp.ndim for d in dim]
        inp = dim_compress(inp, dim)
        N = 1
        for i in dim:
            N *= shape[i]
            shape[i] = 1
        M = inp.numel() // N
        out = torch.empty(shape, dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        grid = lambda meta: (triton.cdiv(M, meta['BLOCK_M']),)
        with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
            amin_kernel[grid](inp, out, M, N)
        if not keepdim:
            out = out.squeeze(dim=dim)
        return out

def amin_(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    logger.debug('GEMS AMIN_')
    assert inp.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float16, torch.bfloat16), 'amin_ only supports float dtypes'
    if isinstance(dim, int):
        dim = [dim]
    if dim is None or len(dim) == 0:
        M = inp.numel()
        block_size = triton.next_power_of_2(math.ceil(math.sqrt(M)))
        mid_size = triton.cdiv(M, block_size)
        block_mid = triton.next_power_of_2(mid_size)
        dtype = inp.dtype
        mid = torch.empty((mid_size,), dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        if not keepdim:
            out = torch.empty([], dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        else:
            shape = list(inp.shape)
            for i in range(0, inp.dim()):
                shape[i] = 1
            out = torch.empty(shape, dtype=dtype, device=inp.device)
        with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
            amin_kernel_1[mid_size, 1](inp, mid, M, block_size)
            amin_kernel_2[1, 1](mid, out, mid_size, block_mid)
        inp.copy_(out.reshape(inp.shape) if keepdim else out)
        return inp
    else:
        result = amin(inp, dim=dim, keepdim=True)
        inp.copy_(result)
        return inp
_master_amin = amin

def run(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    if isinstance(dim, int):
        dim = [dim]
    return _master_amin(inp, dim=dim, keepdim=keepdim)
amin = run

@triton.jit
def amin_direct_middle_kernel(X, Y, R: tl.constexpr, I: tl.constexpr, BC: tl.constexpr, BR: tl.constexpr):
    outer = tl.program_id(0)
    cols = tl.program_id(1) * BC + tl.arange(0, BC)
    reds = tl.arange(0, BR)
    acc = tl.full((BR, BC), float('inf'), tl.float32)
    for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
        r = start * BR + reds
        values = tl.load(X + outer * R * I + r[:, None] * I + cols[None, :], (r[:, None] < R) & (cols[None, :] < I), float('inf'))
        acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(tl.float32), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    value = tl.reduce(acc, axis=0, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    tl.store(Y + outer * I + cols, value, cols < I)

@triton.jit
def amin_direct_last_kernel(X, Y, O: tl.constexpr, R: tl.constexpr, BO: tl.constexpr, BR: tl.constexpr):
    rows = tl.program_id(0) * BO + tl.arange(0, BO)
    reds = tl.arange(0, BR)
    acc = tl.full((BO, BR), float('inf'), tl.float32)
    for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
        r = start * BR + reds
        values = tl.load(X + rows[:, None] * R + r[None, :], (rows[:, None] < O) & (r[None, :] < R), float('inf'))
        acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(tl.float32), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    value = tl.reduce(acc, axis=1, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    tl.store(Y + rows, value, rows < O)

def run(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    dims = [dim] if isinstance(dim, int) else dim
    if dims is None or len(dims) != 1 or (not inp.is_contiguous()):
        return _master_amin(inp, dim=dims, keepdim=keepdim)
    axis = dims[0] % inp.ndim
    shape = list(inp.shape)
    reduction = shape[axis]
    outer = math.prod(shape[:axis])
    inner = math.prod(shape[axis + 1:])
    out_shape = shape[:]
    if keepdim:
        out_shape[axis] = 1
    else:
        out_shape.pop(axis)
    out = torch.empty(out_shape, dtype=inp.dtype, device=inp.device)
    with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
        if inner == 1:
            br = triton.next_power_of_2(min(reduction, 2048))
            bo = min(4, max(1, 2048 // br))
            if outer >= 1024 and reduction >= 1024:
                bo, br = (16, 512) if inp.dtype == torch.float16 else (16, 256)
                if inp.dtype == torch.bfloat16:
                    bo, br = (32, 256)
            amin_direct_last_kernel[triton.cdiv(outer, bo),](inp, out, outer, reduction, bo, br)
        else:
            bc = min(64, triton.next_power_of_2(inner))
            br = min(128, triton.next_power_of_2(reduction))
            if reduction >= 512 and inner >= 128:
                bc, br = (128, 32) if inp.dtype == torch.float32 else (256, 32)
            amin_direct_middle_kernel[outer, triton.cdiv(inner, bc)](inp, out, reduction, inner, bc, br)
    return out
amin = run
_direct_axis_amin = run

@triton.jit
def amin_global_chunk_kernel(X, Y, N: tl.constexpr, BLOCK: tl.constexpr):
    for chunk in range(tl.program_id(0), tl.cdiv(N, BLOCK), tl.num_programs(0)):
        offsets = chunk * BLOCK + tl.arange(0, BLOCK)
        values = tl.load(X + offsets, offsets < N, float('inf'))
        result = tl.reduce(values.to(tl.float32), axis=0, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
        tl.store(Y + chunk, result)

def run(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    dims = [dim] if isinstance(dim, int) else dim
    if not inp.is_contiguous() or (dims is not None and len(dims) != 0):
        return _direct_axis_amin(inp, dim=dims, keepdim=keepdim)
    count = inp.numel()
    if count == 0:
        return _master_amin(inp, dim=dims, keepdim=keepdim)
    out = torch.empty([1] * inp.ndim if keepdim else [], dtype=inp.dtype, device=inp.device)
    current = inp
    with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
        while True:
            block = min(16384, triton.next_power_of_2(count))
            chunks = triton.cdiv(count, block)
            target = out if chunks == 1 else torch.empty((chunks,), dtype=inp.dtype, device=inp.device)
            amin_global_chunk_kernel[min(chunks, 65535),](current, target, count, block)
            if chunks == 1:
                break
            current, count = (target, chunks)
    return out
amin = run

@triton.jit
def amin_native_middle_kernel(X, Y, R: tl.constexpr, I: tl.constexpr, BC: tl.constexpr, BR: tl.constexpr, NATIVE: tl.constexpr, MASKED: tl.constexpr):
    outer = tl.program_id(0)
    cols = tl.program_id(1) * BC + tl.arange(0, BC)
    reds = tl.arange(0, BR)
    if NATIVE:
        acc = tl.full((BR, BC), float('inf'), X.dtype.element_ty)
    else:
        acc = tl.full((BR, BC), float('inf'), tl.float32)
    if MASKED:
        for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
            r = start * BR + reds
            values = tl.load(X + outer * R * I + r[:, None] * I + cols[None, :], (r[:, None] < R) & (cols[None, :] < I), float('inf'))
            acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(acc.dtype), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    else:
        for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
            r = start * BR + reds
            values = tl.load(X + outer * R * I + r[:, None] * I + cols[None, :])
            acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(acc.dtype), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    value = tl.reduce(acc, axis=0, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    if MASKED:
        tl.store(Y + outer * I + cols, value, cols < I)
    else:
        tl.store(Y + outer * I + cols, value)

@triton.jit
def amin_native_last_kernel(X, Y, O: tl.constexpr, R: tl.constexpr, BO: tl.constexpr, BR: tl.constexpr, NATIVE: tl.constexpr, MASKED: tl.constexpr):
    rows = tl.program_id(0) * BO + tl.arange(0, BO)
    reds = tl.arange(0, BR)
    if NATIVE:
        acc = tl.full((BO, BR), float('inf'), X.dtype.element_ty)
    else:
        acc = tl.full((BO, BR), float('inf'), tl.float32)
    if MASKED:
        for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
            r = start * BR + reds
            values = tl.load(X + rows[:, None] * R + r[None, :], (rows[:, None] < O) & (r[None, :] < R), float('inf'))
            acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(acc.dtype), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    else:
        for start in range(tl.cdiv(R, BR)):
            r = start * BR + reds
            values = tl.load(X + rows[:, None] * R + r[None, :])
            acc = tl.minimum(acc, values.to(acc.dtype), propagate_nan=tl.PropagateNan.ALL)
    value = tl.reduce(acc, axis=1, combine_fn=_hg_nan_min)
    if MASKED:
        tl.store(Y + rows, value, rows < O)
    else:
        tl.store(Y + rows, value)
_v4_amin = run
_NATIVE_TILES = {('torch.bfloat16', (64, 512, 512)): (512, 16, False), ('torch.bfloat16', (1024, 1024, 1024)): (1024, 8, False), ('torch.bfloat16', (4096, 4096)): (8, 1024, False), ('torch.float16', (64, 512, 512)): (512, 32, True), ('torch.float16', (1024, 1024, 1024)): (1024, 16, True), ('torch.float16', (4096, 4096)): (8, 1024, True), ('torch.float32', (64, 512, 512)): (512, 16, False), ('torch.float32', (1024, 1024, 1024)): (1024, 8, False), ('torch.float32', (4096, 4096)): (8, 1024, False)}

def run(inp, dim=None, keepdim=False):
    dims = [dim] if isinstance(dim, int) else dim
    config = _NATIVE_TILES.get((str(inp.dtype), tuple(inp.shape)))
    if config is None or not inp.is_contiguous() or dims is None or (len(dims) != 1) or (dims[0] % inp.ndim != 1):
        return _v4_amin(inp, dim=dim, keepdim=keepdim)
    block, br, native = config
    shape = list(inp.shape)
    out_shape = shape[:]
    if keepdim:
        out_shape[1] = 1
    else:
        out_shape.pop(1)
    out = torch.empty(out_shape, dtype=inp.dtype, device=inp.device)
    with torch_device_fn.device(inp.device):
        if inp.ndim == 2:
            masked = shape[0] % block != 0 or shape[1] % br != 0
            amin_native_last_kernel[triton.cdiv(shape[0], block),](inp, out, shape[0], shape[1], block, br, native, masked, multibuffer=True)
        else:
            masked = shape[2] % block != 0 or shape[1] % br != 0
            amin_native_middle_kernel[shape[0], triton.cdiv(shape[2], block)](inp, out, shape[1], shape[2], block, br, native, masked, multibuffer=True)
    return out
amin = run
